##### Raw second transcription

```yaml
```yaml
lines:
  - id: household.h_hoh_test1_count
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Number of persons meeting Test 1: your parent whom you can claim as a dependent (except under a multiple support agreement), whose main home for all of 2025 you paid over half the cost of keeping up"
    type: Count
    expr:
      op: sum_over
      collection: dependent
      field: dependent.one
      where: {op: test, eq: [dependent.hoh_test1, {const: true}]}
    note: "Each dependent entry's own Test 1 result (dependent.hoh_test1) is taken as computed on the dependent form; this line only counts the entries for which it is true."
  - id: household.h_hoh_test2_count
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Number of persons meeting Test 2: a person who lived with you in your home for more than half of the year and is one of the persons listed under Test 2"
    type: Count
    expr:
      op: sum_over
      collection: dependent
      field: dependent.one
      where: {op: test, eq: [dependent.hoh_test2, {const: true}]}
    note: "The person conditions of Test 2 (items 1-4, with the exception to time lived with you) are taken from dependent.hoh_test2. The cost-of-keeping-up-your-home condition of Test 2 is tested separately on household.h_hoh_qualifying_person using household.home_cost. If dependent.hoh_test2 already includes that condition, testing it again here does not change the result."
  - id: household.h_hoh_unmarried
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "You are unmarried (or considered unmarried) on the last day of 2025"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      all:
        - op: test
          any:
            - {op: test, eq: [household.married_dec31, {const: false}]}
            - {op: test, eq: [household.considered_unmarried, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [household.spouse_died_this_year, {const: false}]}
    note: "Pub. 501 says that if your spouse died during the year, you are considered married for the whole year for filing status purposes. The married_dec31 question leaves the spouse-death case to be addressed separately, so a spouse's death in 2025 is treated here as failing the unmarried requirement. A registered domestic partner is not married (Pub. 555), so household.rdp is not used."
  - id: household.h_hoh_qualifying_person
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Either Test 1 or Test 2 applies"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      any:
        - {op: test, gt: [household.h_hoh_test1_count, {const: 0, type: Count}]}
        - op: test
          all:
            - {op: test, eq: [household.home_cost, {const: true}]}
            - {op: test, gt: [household.h_hoh_test2_count, {const: 0, type: Count}]}
    note: "Test 1 needs over half the cost of keeping up the parent's home, which is not necessarily your own home. That condition belongs to the dependent entry (dependent.parent_home_paid feeding dependent.hoh_test1), so household.home_cost applies only to Test 2."
  - id: household.hoh_fails
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Head of household is checked but you are not unmarried (or considered unmarried) with Test 1 or Test 2 applying"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      all:
        - {op: test, eq: [f1040.status, {const: "hoh"}]}
        - op: test
          not:
            op: test
            all:
              - {op: test, eq: [household.h_hoh_unmarried, {const: true}]}
              - {op: test, eq: [household.h_hoh_qualifying_person, {const: true}]}
    note: "The IRS pages do not name this interface. It is read as: the Head of household box is checked (i1040gi: check it only if you are unmarried or considered unmarried and either Test 1 or Test 2 applies), and those requirements are not met. It is false whenever another filing status is chosen."

Second transcription's note: The IRS pages do not name this interface. It is read as: the Head of household box is checked (i1040gi: check it only if you are unmarried or considered unmarried and either Test 1 or Test 2 applies), and those requirements are not met. It is false whenever another filing status is chosen.
